namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ argha vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of the offering water.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ gan dha a va bhā sa na svāhā

This is the mantra of scent.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ puṣpa vajra agra dha ra ka svāhā

This is the mantra of flowers.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ jñā na vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of incense.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ akṣa ya dharmma dhā tu spha ra ṇa vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of the food offering.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ tathāgate cakṣur vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of the lamp.

This is the treasury of mantras for the methods of the maṇḍala.

The bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, again paid homage to all the tathāgatas, arose from that samādhi, and entered into the samādhi called Lofty Vajra, Crest of the Lofty Secret of the Great Vajra. As soon as the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, was settled in equipoise, all the tathāgatas gave him the vajra-syllable heart mantra called Power of the Splendor of All the Tathāgatas, which bears the words "Vajra Upsurge":

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ t ṭa ta ṭa spho ṭa ya vajra saṃ kṛ ta vi dyu ji hvā du rdānta da ma ka da ha si ta laṃ vo dar ka ha ka ha cu lu cu lu muñca aṭṭa hā sa na dā ra vi dā ra vajra vaktra vajra aṃ ga vajra nai tra vajra śro tra vajra ghrā ṇa vajra daṃ ṣṭa a nu ca ra kiñci rā ya si pra vi śa vajra pā ṇi hṛ da yoṃ ā vi śa ā vi śa ghurṇa ghurṇa ghrurṇā pa ya ghurṇā pa ya sarva ta thā ga ta ā jñā ka ra pra va ra dharmma labdha vajrā ya hūṃ kṛ ta svāhā

As soon as all the tathāgatas had spoken the vajra heart mantra called Upsurge, the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was satisfied and gratified, overjoyed and delighted, and at that moment he entered into the samādhi called Vajra Fire. As soon as the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was settled in equipoise in this samādhi, at that moment all the world systems moved, moved strongly, moved strongly everywhere; they quaked, quaked strongly, quaked strongly everywhere. All the great oceans were stirred, strongly stirred, strongly stirred everywhere. All the Mount Merus, kings of mountains, quaked, quaked strongly, quaked strongly everywhere.

There was rumbling, strong rumbling, strong rumbling everywhere. All the world systems blazed, blazed strongly, blazed strongly everywhere, and appeared to have become a single mass of flame. The bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi too blazed, blazed strongly, blazed strongly everywhere, and, taking on the form of a Great Terrifying One, he illuminated the ten directions with rays of blazing flame from his vajra eyes. Then he brought forth the wrathful one called Vajra Great Terrifying One, blazing, strongly blazing, spreading and blazing strongly everywhere, become a single mass of flame, three-eyed, four-fanged, laughing with a horse's neigh, wearing on his body the bloody hide of a great elephant, and holding a vajra in his hand.

Furthermore, from all the pores of his body and from all the joints of his limbs he manifested all the vajra wrathful ones, of unlovely and terrifying form, holding vajras in their hands; of various colors; making their sound heard throughout all the world systems; holding tridents in their hands; terrifying all who are hostile; destroying all the abodes of Māra; gnashing, laughing, giving out booming sounds and rumbling sounds; brandishing tridents in their hands; holding waisted pestles; holding clubs in their hands; holding swords in their hands; holding hammers in their hands; holding axes in their hands; holding in their hands single-pointed spears, lances, great arrows, wheels, iron arrows, nooses, staffs, iron chains, needle-like weapons, ploughs, drums, gongs, bells, and great drums; outshining the splendor of all who possess splendor; and intent on watching over the pledge of the tathāgatas.

Again, from his crown protuberance he manifested the king of wrathful ones called Trailokyavijaya, arisen from the domain of all the tathāgatas, ablaze with blazing flames, with a topknot and a pendulous belly, having a body the size of ten great trichiliocosms, like the fire at the time of the world's destruction, his forehead creased with rows of wrathful furrows, wearing a lower garment of the bloody hide of the king of beasts, seated and ready to receive commands.

Again, at the center of his forehead he manifested everywhere another great king of wrathful ones called Wrathful Candratilaka, with many hundreds of thousands of arms, three-eyed, four-fanged, uttering the sound of a horse's neighing laughter, resembling Yama in form, accompanied by a retinue of many hundreds of thousands of wrathful attendants, holding various weapons in his hands, wearing a skirt of tiger skin, and bringing all sentient beings under his power.

Again, Vajrapāṇi arose from that samādhi and manifested the wrathful one Vajra Mahādaṇḍa, his belly like the belly of a wolf, with red eyes, full of wrath, blue like sapphire, holding a club in his hand, fierce and terrifying, wearing a skirt of scorpion skin, and making the threatening gesture.

Again, he entered into equipoise in the samādhi called Supreme Strength, and having done so, he manifested the great king of wrathful ones called Mahābala, four-armed, his hair standing on end, with yellow eyes and four fangs, like a summer cloud, holding a club in his hand and holding an axe in his hand, pot-bellied, three-eyed, wearing a skirt of tiger skin, dwelling amid the blazing flames of his own body.

Again, Vajrapāṇi entered into equipoise in the samādhi of overpowering with the stride of the Tathāgata, and manifested the wrathful one called Great Vajra of the Stride, his hair loose and standing on end, resembling Yama in form, his face and forehead creased with rows of wrathful furrows, with red eyes, laughing loudly, his mouth gaping, four-armed, holding in his hands a trident, a vajra, a wheel and a sword, gazing in the ten directions.

Again, Vajrapāṇi entered into equipoise in the samādhi called Arising of the Fierce Vajra, and manifested this great queen of knowledge, Vajraśṛṅkhalā, unfailing and unobstructed, who utterly bewilders the three worlds, who produces fearlessness in all sentient beings, arisen from all the tathāgatas, dark blue in color like the priyaṅgu flower, bearing the vajra emblem, ablaze with garlands of flame, her face as if her mind were enraged, holding a vajra chain in her hand, intent on obeying commands, and accompanied by a retinue of ten thousand messenger women.

Again, he manifested Vajra Peak, who has definitively realized all phenomena, of unobstructed power, who tames all hostile sentient beings, of great power, holding a vajra peak in her hand, her face creased with rows of wrathful furrows, of the age of a woman in her middle years, six-armed, holding in her hands a noose, a pestle, a hammer, a waisted pestle and a thorn, and accompanied by a retinue of ten thousand messenger women.

Again, he manifested the queen of knowledge Vajramuṣṭi, arisen from the Dharma fist of all the tathāgatas, bearing the vajra emblem, blazing intensely, with light like the rays of the autumn moon, three-eyed, a goddess with a topknot, wearing a skirt of white cotton, who utterly crushes all the gods and asuras who are hard to subdue.

These are the heart mantras of those wrathful ones:

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra jvāla anala sambhava traṭa traṭa sphoṭaya sphoṭaya hūṃ krodha svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Great Terrifying One.

namaḥ sarvathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā he trailokya vijaya ha ha svāhā

This is the mantra of Trailokyavijaya.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kaṭa kaṭa maṭa maṭa matha matha krodhendu tilka svāhā

This is the mantra of Wrathful Candratilaka.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathāte nīla vajra daṇḍa kiñci rāyasi hana daha bhañja tathāgata samayamanusmaraṇa svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Nīladaṇḍa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā mahābala rakṣa avalokita vajra adhika hana vidhvanasaya hūṃ hūṃ svāhā

This is the mantra of Mahābala.

namaḥ sarva tathagātebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā tathāgata nodārya ana avalokite vajra mardha vajroṣṇīṣa hūṃ hūṃ prajvālita mūrdhi svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajroṣṇīṣa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bhagavate vajraṃ śṛkhale bandha bandha moṭṭa moṭṭa saṃkocaya saṃkocaya sarva sandi hri varade svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajraśṛṅkhalā.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bhinda bhinda sarva duṣṭa namaḥ sarvatra apratihate bhagavati vajra acivara he helini svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Peak.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā gatodbhave jātini trilocane sarva dharmandhir dhani kiti saṃkarani svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajramuṣṭi.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra patāṃke vajra manase svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Banner.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā he vajra mālani vajra pāṇaye bahu rājā mayi svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Garland.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra kula sundhari vajraṃgi citariṇi purari svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajrakulasundarī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ajā jālini vajra triśūlini hi hi he he svahā

This is the mantra of Vajra Trident Bearer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ākarṣaya sarvama bhagavate vajrāṃkuśi kiri kiri miri miri he hale svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajrāṅkuśī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā tathāgate vāgatra sambhave vajra daṃṣṭro khādaya sarva kuru tathāgata ājrāṃ ritini svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Fang.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā sarva dharma prabhedana kara vajra mudgara hili hiṭha svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Hammer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra prākāra tathāgate bhañjanaka kuru kuru cakreṇa svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Fence.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā tathāgate viskambha vajra kīla kīla kara svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Dagger.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra pañjarani ka kṣipa gagana khaṃ svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Net.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hāsa vajra jala jala jala svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Laughter.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra vilāsa lalita ananta svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Charm.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvatha tathāgata nirghoṣa vajra dundubhi hās svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Great Drum.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra ghaṇṭe bhagavatijñāna rasmis bhūte hari svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Bell.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra roma mani niritini svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Body Hair.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra buli nakhi śikhini svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Clawed One.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dhara dūti cipita mukha gaccha gaccha kuru tathāgata ājñā svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Messenger.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrod bhave vajra vajra śikha vicitri śvari svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Crown.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bhagavati vajra karāli vajra piśāci jijiri svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Piśācī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vasta karāla vajra ānan vajra piśāca raṃ kuru svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Piśāca.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra rakṣasi khādaya sarva tathāgata saṃbhave śamakari vajra cana svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Rākṣasa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā amahā vajra khaḍga cchinda sarva gacaṃ sarva satvānāna tathāgata saṃga saṃbhana svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Sword.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā tathāgata koṣa saṃbhava bhūta vaśi prāpta svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajrodbhava.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra anala bindu jihvā vajra rākṣasi svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Rākṣasī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā cchāya gata sarva gati tathāgata cchāya saṃbhava rakṣasa vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Shadow Oppressor.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā antattaya vināśāna vikṛta rauṃ drakśa vajra satva svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Oppressor.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hara hara vajra vajra tila piṃgala locana kāla kāla svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Apasmāra.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā devaṃ ga bu gata tathāgata ājñā kara piva tha se pithā vajra satvā svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Deva.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra nāga mukhi gauri nīla titi nī svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Nāga.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā dhara dhara vajra dhara yakṣa kula sundhara svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Yakṣa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vikara vajra śikhara gagana cariṇi svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Gandharva.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā tana saṃbhava vajra mathana ṭema bhici svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Asura.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā khana jaritire reṭiri vajra mukha svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Garuḍa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra gauri ṭikiṭini svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Mahoraga.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā rāga dārāyāna na jaliva vajra rakṣa mālyaṃ vara dhara vajra pāṇi priya svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Red Body.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvatha kili kili kalo karālā vagra sara sara gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Kīlikīla.

namaḥ sarva tathagatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra vidār pūraya pūraya vaha vaha svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Destroyer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā he laṃgili kurdani ha he kihi svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Glider.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā halahu narakupara vadhara dhara vidhara vidhara kadha kadha kathā vajrapāṇi vajrapriya kaha kaha ha ha he he kiñca rāyasi sahasra bhuja sahasra ānana bindu daṃṣṭra durdānta damaka prajvalita keśa nakharo madśana dali bindu sūrita jihva candra ātitya mukuṭa goti vircita śikhara vajra gada keyūra kuṇḍalaṃ alaṃkṛta śarīra vajra nakha vajra aṃgulaṃ vajra hasta vajra carana vajra gātra vajra netra nīla vajra vajra gata vaḍava mukha gani gambhāṃra hūṃkāra ratita sata hasita lambodara grasa grasa praviśa āviśa āviśa ghurṇa ghurṇa ghurṇapaya ghūrṇāpaya sarva satva bhūma bhūma bhūmaya bhūmaya sarva duṣṭa paca paca daha daha matha matha hūṃ hūṃ vajrapāṇi ājñāpayati svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Nadakubera.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā pracala mobuli vidhara vajra kriha nakha vajrasatva svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Vidyādhara.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā śuddha vajra prākṛti śuddha śodhaya sarva svāhā

This is the vajra mantra that purifies all substances.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra agni vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Fire.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra anala pracala mauli thiddya dar svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Wind Blaze.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajraṃga vajra dhara jina ādhara svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Earth.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā viraje vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Free of Dust.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā smṛti nadada vajrasatva svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Mindfulness.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā padma vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Lotus.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarva ṭra vajrottama karuṇa agadhari kāruṇa maṇḍala svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Compassion.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā maitryudgata sarva satva hitaṃkata svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Loving-Kindness.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā pāramitā garbha sarvatra apratihata vajra cinta svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Perfection.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā sarva śūnyatā vajra nita viśuddha satva averya vajra hṛdaya svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Emptiness.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dhāraṇī śvara dhara vajra svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Dhāraṇī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra bodhyaṃga dṛḍha āśāya sarva buddha svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Limb of Awakening.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā mārga viṣadhaka dhaka dhaka cara cara svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Path.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā satya vajra samuṅgata buddha rūbadhar svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Truth.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā avenika vajra saṃbhava buddha putra svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Unshared.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā abhīṣa radva vajra mārga nidariṣaka svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Fearlessness.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā triratna sarva paka buddha rūpaṇi dara svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Three Jewels.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā dṛḍha mūla vajra vṛkṣa bodha nidada svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Tree of Awakening.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā aparājita vajra jitari la bhadrānu jñāna svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Aparājita.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra ratnākāri svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Ratnākara.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā pralaya vajra jva jvala dhaka svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Traceless.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra vivadhaya sarva hūṃ hūṃ svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Formation.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā saṃbhyaḥ sarva vajra vajrottama karuṇa māya svāhā

This is the mantra of Terrifying by the Vajra.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā saṃpatti vajra samaya svāhā

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra kumāra vajra hasta svāhā

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kāma vajra vilasini takiti svāhā

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kāma vajra vidyādhari kumude svāhā

This is the mantra of Kāmavajra the Vidyādhara.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā rakṣa vajra nika manda de kāma yakṣaṇi svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Yakṣiṇī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra mataṃgi khiri khiga kharika svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Caṇḍālī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā rudhira priya rudhira vetāli lihi laha liligini vajra mathini rahi

This is the mantra of Vajra Blood Vetālī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra pitāli kali māli nava māli luśudha svarihe svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Vetālī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dunte vajra śītike udpādani pāda mā vilamba vajrapāṇi ājñāpayati svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Wrathful One.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kunakhi kunakhi vajrapāṇi priye hiti hide svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Kunakhī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrāṃgi kuruṃgani vajra tundeke svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Plumed Lady.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dhavali dhalitaṃgi vajra bhramarike svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Bee.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra śavari śavari rūpa dhāraṇi ghini ghiniṇike svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Śabarī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra vasanatike vajra kusum hasta vajra kusuma sambhave svahā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Flower.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hāsa hāsa vajra hasantu nṛtya nṛtya svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Laughter.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hi mada valige vajra madava vajrapāṇi manusmara svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Taste.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra vajra kukku rima samukhi vajrod bhave auya kiṃ dande devi vajra pataki svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Bitch.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrodpi lakehi nadandi vini vikuni svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Utpalā.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra mālini khailanatike cala cala vicala vicala ruṭṭu lalu ru svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Playful Lady.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra haraṇa khādaya sarva dura nivariṇi hare svāhā

This is the mantra of Vajra Doe.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra maṃkati rakta mukhi rakta akṣi vajrotti kadda kadda ma pilamba vajrapāṇi rājñāpayati svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Monkey.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dharaṇi dhara dhara saṃdhara svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajradharā.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra kālike drametiko randika haṭa huṭe svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Kālī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā thalaṃva vigalita mahā maṇi kutvaṃsa svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Seated in Pride.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā pracaṇḍa vajra dharaka nirdayi daha daha bhañja bhañja samayamanusmara svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Pracaṇḍa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra lalita vajrapāṇi priya sarva satva vaśaṃkari svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajralalita.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra candra mukhe kha kha khāhi khāhi smara vratijñā svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Moon Face.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra gona durdānta damaka vimala mukha jahi jahi svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Stainless.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra baladhari sisimi svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Strength Holder.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā khecara vajra gamana he ha ha svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Sky Goer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajraṃ gato tara tara duvar vara cara cara kama kama svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Noble.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrottama svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Supreme.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra vāktra duha duha svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Face.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dandura capiṭa vajra rana silada hari lambodara dhara dhuvara svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Tooth.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā raṃ jaya jaya vajra rutra jaya jaya caṇḍa roṣaṇa svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Wrathful One.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā trailokya grasana vajra duha vajra pāśa hasta ānaya ānaya kiñci rāyasi tathāgata samayam nusmara svāhā

This is the mantra of the messenger Vajra Three Worlds.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā caṇḍala vajra kiṃkara kuru sarvaṃ kiñci rāyasi vicitra phatiṅ parite svahā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Caṇḍa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra piṅgala lalita keśa prajvalita śarīra hā kadhaka kuru kuru svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Piṅgala.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra ketu kiṅkara dhara dhara thara thara kara kara svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajraketu.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā te gadhilaka kiñca rāya lalita veṣa dhari dhāraṇi dhāraṇi pratijñā muhu muhu muñca dahasaṃ svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Great Time.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra kula ājñā kara rata rata riṃvi svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Doer of Commands.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ha kṛtānda śamana bhañja kalaha priya hili hili lalita dusta sarva hṛdaya svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Lover of Strife.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra śikhara mulu mulu helu helu svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Peak.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kata kaṭa matha maṭa hūṃ hūṃ vajra kula sundhara dhara hara hara svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajrakulasundara.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hala hala ṭara ṭara luntana vajra kiṅkara svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Ruiner.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dhatañjaya jaṃkara butantara hva svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Victor over Wind.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvatha kotina jaya kiñca rāyasi gṛhṇa gṛhṇa koti tiṅgati gata svāhā

This is the mantra of the attendant Vajra Victor over Extremes.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā capalike capala vajrīṇi svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Capalā.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā su vajrīṇi vajra dutike svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Excellent.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra pañjarike vajrini svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Net Lady.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ratna mālike vajrapāṇi priye svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Jewel Garland.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra mālini dapala mukhi vajrīṇi svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra White Lady.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra lambike svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Lambikā.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra suganatige vajragi vajra dayitaka svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Beloved.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā sutiṣṭha vajrīṇi vajrapāṇi hṛdayaṃ game svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Well-Abiding.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra dukula bhakṣe śu harani svahā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Wearer of Dukūla Cloth.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra karalike vartike svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Even Lady.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra jayakari vajra śarīrāṇi svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Jayakarī.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra karabhi vajra atiśanike svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Threatener.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra vajriṇi vajra vadhike svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Slayer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā mekhala lalita keśa vari vajra gandhari svāhā

This is the mantra of the maidservant Vajra Word Holder.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā susiddhi kara vajra sādhakayi sarva vajrapāṇi rājñā kuru svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Susiddhikara.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ratna māli vajra dhara svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Three-Pointed Jewel Diadem.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vidyottama cinta vajra hūṃ

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Vidyottama.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bala kilikila vajra makuṭa dhara svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Bala Kīlikīla.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathāratna keli kila ratna makuṭa dhara svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Ratna Kīlikīla.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā surūpa māla vajra vajra hitaṃkara svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Surūpa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra viṃnadu gata vajra dhara svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Vajra Bearer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā lalita vajra puruṣa mahā yakṣa rūpa dharas rasara svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Vajralalita.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā gaja karṇa vajra locana caṃna calakā svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Gajakarṇa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā traṭa vajra kañja hūṃ svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Fair Harmer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bhīṣaṇa vajra bhīma vajrapāṇi rājñāpayati svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Bhīṣaṇa.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hariti sarvatra me pratihata tara tara svāhā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Vajra Seizer.

namaḥ sarva tathāgatebhyaḥ sarva mukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hāsa vajra dhara sutānu sarva arta sartaka hi hi ri svahā

This is the mantra of the king of knowledge Vajrahāsa.

The Tantra of the Empowerment of Vajrapāṇi, the fifth fascicle.

Then again Vajrapāṇi entered into equipoise in the samādhi called Source of All Limbs, and through the power of that samādhi, sons born of his heart and issuing from his mouth emerged from his mind and from all his limbs. From his two hands came the youth Subāhu. From his mouth came the youth Vajrasena. From his vajra diadem came the youth Vajra Diadem. From the crown of his head came the youth Vajra Crown-Born. From the vajra flowers came the youth Vajra Flower. From the vajra flower chaplet came the youth Vajra Flower Chaplet. From the vajra chest came the youth Vajra Chest. From the vajra heart came the youth Vajra Dear One. From the vajra forehead came the youth Vajra Moon. From the vajra eyebrows came the youth Vajra Eyebrow. From the vajra ears came the youth Vajradhara. From the vajra eyes came the youth Vajra Single Eye of the World. From the vajra nose came the youth Vajra Bearer of Force. From the vajra cheeks came the youth Vajra Support. From the vajra chin came the youth Vajra Holder of the Cherished. From the vajra teeth came the youth Vajra Fang. From the vajra neck came the youth Vajra Song. From the vajra armpits came the youth Vajra Splendor of Blazing Fire. From the back came the youth Vajra Yoke Bearer.

Thus these and the other wrathful youths, sons of the Blessed One Vajrapāṇi, bearing vajras in their hands, with minds devoted to the welfare of sentient beings, arisen from the maṇḍala of compassion, drawn forth by the aspiration of Vajrapāṇi, who in various world systems have fully and perfectly awakened to supreme and highest awakening, whose bodies are produced from meditative absorption, dhāraṇī, liberation, the grounds, the perfections, the powers, the limbs of awakening, the noble path, and great emptiness, and who fulfill the wishes of all sentient beings, were every one of them dear to the heart of Vajrapāṇi.

Then Vajrapāṇi, Lord of Secrets, arose from that meditative absorption and spoke these secret mantras:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrasenoddhava vajrapāṇipriya svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Army.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrapahūdbha vajrabahuśuraṃgama svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Subāhu.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bicitra vajraśekhara vajraśikharāpilambita hara svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Flower Chaplet.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kiriṭi vajradhari viśuddha aṃgara svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Diadem.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajramūrbha agita hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Born from the Crown.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kusuma acidrata vajramurti vajrasvabhāva svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Flower.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajravaradhari uravajrotatama svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Breast.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrahṛdaya vajrapāṇipriya satvahitaṃkara svāhā
This is the mantra of the youth Vajra Dear One.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā candravajra aṃgasundhara svāhā
This is the mantra of the youth Vajra Moon.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajracakramaṇḍala saṃbuta vajrabhruva svāhā
This is the mantra of the youth Vajra Eyebrow.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra aṃkudhari śrutādhara dhara nirdada svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Holder of the Supreme Ear.

namaḥ sarvatathagatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajralocana jñānacakṣurdada svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Eye.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajravegadhari manojavedhara dhara svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Bearer of Force.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā uddhara vajrasamayamnupālaya svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Support.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajradhara dhara jvālitamūrti svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Holder of the Cherished.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajra aṃkura vajraketu sara sara svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Fang.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā saṃbodhi vajradharapriya vajragīti svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Song.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrajvalita vajramūrti vajradhara svāhā
This is the mantra of the youth Vajra Blazing.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā teja avalambita mūrti dhaka dhaka svāhā
This is the mantra of the youth Vajra Yoke Bearer.

Then Vajrapāṇi, who carries out the command of all the tathāgatas, empowered the meditative absorption of Māyā, arisen from the vajra, the mother of all buddhas and bodhisattvas; and, bearing the great cord of bodily protection that wards off all harm, he uttered the sound that declares "mine," as one who says, "Let it never be done!"

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā he vajraśūlini vajramataṃgi anantamukha vajrahili jayantiṃ ruti mukhati si sphuranti svāhā
This is the mantra of Māmakī.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bubimo vajramālīni vajra adabhūte sañcaya samantaśītiśama vajrarandhanena ghudhu ghudhu jahi jahi mābilamda bhagavate bārāhī smara smara vajrasaṃgīti svāhā
This is the mantra of the vajra boundary.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajramahāmaṇḍa darśaya vajrī bikrama svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Holder of the Essence.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrasitātapatre jvakṛta svāhā
This is the mantra of the Vajra White Parasol.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bitata mahāvajra bidana raṃga raṃgāpaya svahā
This is the mantra of the Vajra Great Jewel Banner.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ratnakūṭa jvalita vajrodabhava svāhā
This is the mantra of the Vajra Multistoried Mansion.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrapadmoddhava vajra āsana vajranadada svāhā
This is the mantra of the Vajra Lotus Seat.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā smṛti vajradharaka svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Mindfulness.

namaḥ sarvatathagatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvatha bodhi bibodhana vajra bama bamaba svahā
This is the mantra of Vajra Awakening.

namaḥ sarvatathagatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvatha pimati haraka vajraratna dhama dhama svaha
This is the mantra called Vajra Cutter of Doubt.

namaḥ sarvatathagatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā jñānavajra svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Giver of Knowledge.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvatha amṛtavajrapa svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Nectar.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā pravartaya vajrajñānasāgaraṃra svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Proclaimer of the Dharma.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrasaṃsathāpriya birāgakoṭi svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Free of Attachment.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā śūnyatākoṭi vajrasaṃdharaka svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Emptiness.

namaḥ sarvatathagatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā sarvadharmma samatā prabaka vajrasatva svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Equality.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā gagana gañja nitara śiśaya makhaṃ vajra svāhā
This is the mantra of Vajra Gaganagañja.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajrayakṣaṇi vajradade atcipara ṇi manasi vajramālini svāhā
This is the mantra of the Vajra Yakṣiṇī.

Then Vajrapāṇi arose from that meditative absorption, and again he entered into the meditative absorption called Burning Koṭis with the Host That Strikes at the Life of All Sentient Beings, the śrāvakas, all the pratyekabuddhas, the gods, nāgas, yakṣas, gandharvas, asuras, garuḍas, kiṃnaras, mahoragas, humans, nonhumans and the rest. Then he spoke this supreme vajra essence called the Great Fire of Destruction, which blazes like the seven suns that appear at the time of the great destruction of the world, and which, like the plume of fire in the great island of the ocean, devours the minds and the hosts of mental factors of all sentient beings:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā phaṭ hūṃ

As soon as the essence of the Great Fire of Destruction had been spoken, that entire assembly shook like a grove of plantain trees cut at the root, as if tossed by a great and violent wind, and from all their seats they were thrown face down onto the vajra maṇḍala of the great wheel. Only the bodhisattva great beings who abide on the ten grounds were excepted. Through the might of Vajrapāṇi, that entire assembly saw this whole buddhafield, Meru and great Meru, the Cakravāla range and the great Cakravāla range, blazing and become a single flame, and all the oceans and great oceans dried up, utterly burned, and turned to ash.

From the heart of Vajrapāṇi there then came forth the king of wrathful ones called the Great Flame of Destruction. Letting out a great, loud horse-laugh, he gathered up the hearts of the entire assembly, became equal to space, and entered the maṇḍala of Vajrapāṇi's heart. And Vajrapāṇi, abiding in the maṇḍala of great compassion, at that time abided in the abiding of equality with all the tathāgatas.

Then those tathāgatas said to Vajrapāṇi, "Excellent!" and declared:

"This essence of the ocean of mantra conduct,
Possessed of great power,
Protects this teaching,
And in this abode of the Great Vehicle
Ever tames those who are hard to tame:
Great hero, you have spoken it."

Then the youthful Mañjuśrī, knowing that the entire assembly had fallen onto the vajra maṇḍala of the great wheel, felt great compassion arise. Gazing long with both his eyes, he blessed them with this great gift, the sacred essence of Vajra Supreme. Looking upon the great vajra maṇḍala for the sake of the maṇḍala of supreme great conduct, he said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi, who has attained victory in the supreme sacred Dharma of all the tathāgatas: "Son of good family, grant this assembly some small door for entering the pledge. Through it they will gain hope of life in this teaching, gain faith, and come to offer each their own secret mantra essences."

When he had said this, the bodhisattva great being Vajrapāṇi said to the youthful Mañjuśrī:

"You who have gained mastery over meditative absorption,
Unattached like space,
Who always fulfill the aspirations
Of all the buddhas everywhere:
Tell the conduct of all secret mantras,
And likewise the accomplishment of secret mantras,
The observances, the signs and the forms,
The times, the places and the rites."

At that time he gave that vajra, together with the dhāraṇī of the power of Vajra Supreme, to the youthful Mañjuśrī:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ehi mahāvajrakoṭi kuṭila pāpaka aṃkula śikhe kabila keśī taṭi taṭi koṭi koṭi karkkaṭi muṭi mahāthindud lolo duka duka sama sama sara sara bisara bisara sphura sphura uddhotite hari piṃgale caṭi bicaṭi jvāle bijvāle vajraghoṇa vajrasara dṛṣṭa praharaṇe matibhūte ratnatraya atitale vajradhara biśāsma apratihata pramardani svāhā

Then the youthful Mañjuśrī said to that assembly: "Sons of good family, keep in mind this queen of knowledge, Invincible Vajra Who Utterly Stupefies with Fire, and it will bring you benefit and happiness and the gaining of life."

As soon as Vajrapāṇi had spoken this great knowledge mantra, the Vajra of Sacred Giving blessed that assembly, and they spoke with one voice: "Homage! Homage to the Blessed Invincible Vajra Who Utterly Stupefies with Fire!" In that very instant the assembly rose and, gazing at the bodhisattvas Samantabhadra and the others, at the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi and at the youthful Mañjuśrī, they spoke these words:

"Oh, the wisdom! Oh, the joy!
Oh, the knowledge of mantra, beyond conception!
Through it all of us
Now have a protector and the joy of meditative concentration.
With minds at peace and free of knots,
We have taken the Buddha's teaching earnestly to heart.
Into the presence of the buddhas
All of us have come,
And in this king of rites, this great system,
We now offer our essences."

Then Brahmā said to the Blessed One: "Blessed One, I too offer my own essence here, in these many graded divisions of the maṇḍala of the great empowerment of Vajrapāṇi.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā oṃ

Blessed One, this is my supreme essence. It proclaims the four Vedas, establishes the four castes, and lays out the great arrangement endowed with tones. Blessed One, from this the Vedas arise, and the mantras and the rites such as sacrifice arise; like the great ocean, it is the ground of all phenomena."

Vajrapāṇi said:

"Vajrasattva, the great vajra,
Is what is called the vajra oṃ:
Born of love and meditative absorption,
Excellent, utterly free of pride."

Then Śakra (Indra), lord of the gods, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, please accept this essence of mine, which grants power over every kingdom:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bidarāya mauli mauli pihu bihu svāhā

Blessed One, through this essence I act as lord of the gods. Blessed One, if anyone who has entered this great maṇḍala of the vajra wheel wishes to accomplish this essence of mine there, he should first recite it three hundred thousand times before Vajrapāṇi. At the end he should make ten thousand fire offerings of pine kindling sticks together with butter and honey. Blessed One, I shall accomplish all of that for him. After he dies and passes on, he will be born in the higher realms. If he recites it constantly, he will prosper. If, gazing upon Vajrapāṇi and without taking food, he recites it a hundred thousand times, he will gain a kingdom; but one whose pledge is broken is the exception, for he will not succeed."

Then all those nāga kings said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, there is a nāga essence, and through it all of us come under control. We shall grant accomplishments. We shall protect. We shall remove poison.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā re re rātiṃ nāgale liri phudta rijihe svāhā

To accomplish this essence, one should enter a river that flows into the ocean, or a great lake, and recite it ten thousand times. Then, if one sees some nāga coming toward one, or the lake is stirred up, and one remains unafraid of it, one will succeed. Or else, at the end, one should recite it ten thousand times while offering kindling sticks of khadira wood dipped in white mustard seed oil; in that way too one succeeds. Poisons, whether of moving or unmoving things, will then be removed by mere thought, and the practitioner's various other activities will also be done by mere thought."

Then Vaiśravaṇa, the great general of the yakṣas, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I too offer here, in this great maṇḍala of many graded divisions, this essence of mine, which grants practitioners every excellence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā yakṣaśara kiñcirāyasi samayamanusmara debhīme vaiśravaṇapriya svāhā

Blessed One, whoever accomplishes this essence should go alone to a stūpa that holds relics and recite this essence five hundred thousand times. At the end, having made offerings with whatever he can afford, if throughout the night he makes thirty thousand fire offerings of black sesame mixed with curd, honey and ghee, whatever he wishes will be granted. If, without taking food, in secret and in solitude, he keeps a yakṣiṇī in mind and recites it ten thousand times for seven days and nights, a yakṣiṇī will be granted. If he remembers it every day for one year, I shall grant him a treasure."

Then Āṭavaka, the great general of the yakṣas, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "I too offer my own essence here, in this maṇḍala of many graded divisions:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā khimisiri smaśri paripūrayana pratijñāmantake svāhā

Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps this essence of mine in mind, I shall always follow after him. If he wishes to accomplish it, he should go to a mountain peak and recite it three hundred thousand times; and if he makes a hundred thousand fire offerings of white mustard seed mixed with curd, honey and ghee, I grant whatever he wishes."

Then Dhṛtarāṣṭra, the great general of the yakṣas, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I too offer this essence of mine here in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bidhara dhari gandharirisi svāhā

Blessed One, whoever keeps this essence in mind will not meet an untimely death, and will always be surrounded by good fortune. If he wishes to accomplish it, then from the first day of the month until the full moon he should make ten thousand fire offerings each day, with flowers as the oblation. Then, on the day of the full moon, having made offerings with whatever he can afford, if throughout the night he offers rice porridge in the fire, he will succeed, and I grant whatever he wishes."

Then Virūpākṣa, the great general of the yakṣas, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I too offer my own essence here in this great maṇḍala, in order to fulfill every wish:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā śrīdhi dharaya birūpākṣa svāhā

Blessed One, whatever son or daughter of good family constantly keeps this essence of mine in mind, I shall always follow after him, and I shall fulfill all his wishes. If he wishes to accomplish it, he should go to a mountain peak and recite it three hundred thousand times. Then, on the day of the full moon, having made offerings with whatever he can afford, if throughout the night he makes ten thousand fire offerings of guggulu kindling sticks dipped in honey and ghee, he will succeed, and I grant whatever he wishes. Even if he does not succeed, I grant him five fine districts."

Then Jambhala Jalendra said to the Blessed One: "Blessed One, I too offer here in this great maṇḍala the supreme accomplishment of all the aims of practitioners:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kuru curu muru jaṃbha jalodbhava svāhā

Blessed One, whoever constantly recites this essence of mine, I shall accomplish whatever he wishes."

"If he wishes to accomplish it, he should go to a mountain peak at the time of the solstice and recite it a million times; then I grant a kingdom or a great treasure. If he enchants gorocanā by reciting it once and anoints his eyes, whoever he looks at will come under his control. If at night he recites it over his face, keeps something in mind and lies down, he will see in a dream whatever he had in mind. If he wants clothing, then whatever the color of the flower he throws into water, he will obtain clothing of that same color. If he wants small livestock, then for three weeks he should throw flowers, enchanted once with the mantra, so that they strike the reflection of the sun, and he will obtain them. If he recites it constantly, his fortune will grow great."

Then Eight Arms said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, it is like this: I too shall offer my own essence here in these many great maṇḍalas, in order to fulfill every desire:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā cakra dvadva bāhurāja svāhā

Blessed One, whoever accomplishes this essence of mine should perform the protection and then, in a clean spot in a park, beneath an aśoka tree, recite it three hundred thousand times. At the end, having made offerings to the youth with whatever he can afford, if he burns agaru incense throughout the night and recites, I shall reveal myself to him at midnight or at dawn. I grant whatever excellent thing he wishes. If he recites it a hundred thousand times without taking food, a yakṣiṇī will come right there, and she will grant whatever he wishes. If she does not come, she will perish."

Then Pāñcika, the great general of the yakṣas, bowed and said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I too offer my own essence here in this great maṇḍala, in order to protect, guard and shelter practitioners who strive to accomplish secret mantras:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā pañcala pañcamukha pañcikha hrīṃ hrīṃḥ rakṣa rakṣa svāhā

Blessed One, if any son or daughter of good family who has received the pledge in this great maṇḍala wishes to accomplish secret mantras, he should recite this essence of mine once at the very beginning. Then, to protect, guard and shelter him, I shall come into his presence with my retinue of five hundred sons. The sign that I am coming is this: the sweet fragrance of divine incense will waft from the practitioner's body, his strength of enthusiasm will be complete, and, free of all obstructors, he will gain accomplishment with little hardship.

If he wishes to accomplish this essence of mine, he should recite it three hundred thousand times in the presence of the Buddha, the Blessed One. At the end, he should place the butter of a reddish-yellow cow in a copper vessel and recite until it smokes or blazes; if he then drinks it, he will rise up into the sky. If it smokes, he will retain whatever he hears. Even if he does not succeed, all people will be pleased with him. If he recites it constantly, wealth will gather. If he makes a maṇḍala with cow dung that has not fallen to the ground, imagines himself in the presence of the Blessed One and recites it a hundred thousand times, he will succeed, and it will not be otherwise."

Then the asura kings, Vemacitrin and the others, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, we too offer our own essence here in these many great maṇḍalas:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā traṃgi nibodede mālini jatimi hūṃ svāhā

Blessed One, whoever accomplishes this supremely secret, great, fierce and sacred essence of ours should go to the gate of the Glorious Gate and recite this essence six hundred thousand times. At the end, if he makes a hundred thousand fire offerings of poison mixed with blood and mustard seed, all the abodes of the asuras will be left without bolts and without mechanical devices. Then we shall serve him and let that son of good family dwell there. We shall grant him every requisite, and we shall also grant him every elixir practice with mercury and medicinal essences."

Then the gandharva kings, Variegated Garland and the others, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, we too offer this essence of ours here in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā madta mataṃgi svāhā

This is our essence, and through it we grant accomplishments. Through it we are called the gandharva kings. We enjoy the various pleasures of the gods. In short, we are able to do all that is to be done. We dwell in all the abodes of humans and nonhumans. If anyone wishes to accomplish it, he should first make offerings in a mountain cave with whatever he can afford and recite this essence a hundred thousand times; then he will succeed. At the end, if he kindles a fire with turuṣka oil and makes ten thousand fire offerings of agaru kindling sticks, we shall then fulfill his wishes. If he enchants gorocanā with the mantra and makes a mark on his forehead, all people will be pleased with him. If he enchants his palm with the mantra and passes it over his face, all people will be pleased with him."

Then the kiṃnara kings, Druma the kiṃnara king and the others, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, we too offer this essence of ours here in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā titiliphu svāhā

Blessed One, this is our essence, and through it we are called the kiṃnara kings. Sounding cymbals, beating drums and making music, we go everywhere, throughout all world systems, to the feet of all the tathāgatas. In all the abodes of gods and nāgas we are honored by all. Through this essence one forgets nothing at all. If anyone wishes to accomplish it, he should live alone in the wilderness, eating alms food, and recite this essence two hundred thousand times."

"At the end, he should smear a maṇḍala with cow dung that has not fallen to the ground, mixed with the five products of the cow, make offerings with whatever he can afford, and make ten thousand fire offerings with saffron as the oblation. Then he will succeed: in all the abodes of the kiṃnaras he will be honored by all, and he will do whatever he wishes."

Then all the garuḍa kings, Son of Māra and the others, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, we too offer this essence of ours here in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā raṅkāri biṣari biṣeṣaṇi kṛntani kumārikeha svāhā phu phu svāhā

Through this essence of ours we shall establish all the nāgas in their own pledge. Merely by hearing the name of this essence, all the nāgas become our servants: they become frightened, cowed, trembling and without hope. Left without poison, they flee in every direction. Blessed One, if any son or daughter of good family wishes to accomplish this essence, he should enter a great lake and stand submerged up to the throat. If he recites this essence a hundred thousand times, then that lake will dry up in a single day. All the abodes of the nāgas will blaze, and they will show their own forms. They will also make hundreds of other obstructions, but he should not be afraid of them.

Then, when those signs have appeared, at the end he should smear a maṇḍala on the shore of the ocean with that same cow dung that has not fallen to the ground, make offerings to the Blessed One Vajrapāṇi with whatever he can afford, fashion eight nāga kings out of all the poisons and secondary poisons, and then make fire offerings of mustard seeds, smearing each seed with blood. Then all the nāgas will come and say, 'Take what is excellent!' He should not be frightened. He should say this: 'Become my servants!' They will say, 'So be it!' and vanish, and from then on they will do whatever he wishes."

Then all the rākṣasa kings, Terrifying and the others, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, we too offer our own essence here in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kāṃ'ariṇi luṇḍini svāhā

Through this essence of ours we shall steal the splendor and strength of all humans, and we shall tame all sentient beings. In short, Blessed One, in the three worlds with all that moves and does not move, there is nothing at all that is hard for us to obtain. Blessed One, whoever wishes to accomplish this should, in a charnel ground where smoke is rising, eat meat as his food, wear blue clothing, hold a sword in his hand, and recite this essence a hundred thousand times. At the end he should make a torma offering for all the bhūtas. Having made offerings with whatever he can afford, he should recite until midnight. Then some lord of the rākṣasas will come. Without fearing him, he should offer him water for the guest and say, 'Grant me the accomplishment of taking whatever form I desire.' The rākṣasa will take that sword and give it back to him, and he will then be able to travel as the gods do and will perform various wondrous deeds."

Then the great sage Agni said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I too offer this essence of mine here in this great maṇḍala, to benefit practitioners:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kapīla jyotiniraṇada biṅgala locana svāhā

This essence of mine fulfills every hope. Through it I too am summoned and come under control. I shall increase good fortune and grant wealth. If one recites it constantly, wealth will gather. If with this essence one constantly makes fire offerings of food, food will appear. In short, whatever substance one offers in the fire, that substance will appear. If he wishes to accomplish it, he should go to a mountain peak, eat alms food, and recite this essence three hundred thousand times. At the end, having made offerings with whatever he can afford, he should prepare a fire pit and make ten thousand fire offerings of juniper kindling sticks dipped in curd, honey and ghee. Then he will succeed and will perform the rites as he pleases."

Then the wind gods, Cloud Garland and the others, bowed with joined palms toward where the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was and said to him: "Blessed One, we too offer our own essence here in this great maṇḍala. We shall fulfill all the wishes of practitioners who wish to gain accomplishment:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bhramara bhramare capali capalike herambini svāhā

This is our supreme essence, and through it we carry out the command of the tathāgatas. We shall act in accordance with the scriptural instructions given to bodhisattvas who engage in bodhisattva conduct through the gateway of secret mantra. We shall bring about benefit and happiness. If one who engages in bodhisattva conduct through the gateway of secret mantra first recites this essence three hundred thousand times, we shall then permit him every activity. If anyone wishes to accomplish it, he should recite it ten thousand times without taking food. At the end, having made offerings with whatever he can afford, he should make five maṇḍalas with cow dung that has not fallen to the ground and set up seven banners of various colors. Then, if he recites until the banners stir, he will succeed, and he will perform at will rites such as raising and stopping the wind."

Then the Moon, king of the stars, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I too offer my own essence here in this great maṇḍala, which completely pacifies all torment and firmly establishes good fortune:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā candrakānati biśuddha haṃga svāhā

Blessed One, this essence of ours completely pacifies all the torments of those who engage in bodhisattva conduct through the gateway of secret mantra."

If one recites it constantly, it dispels everything inauspicious. It increases glory. It constantly bestows good fortune. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should take milk as one's only food, enter a lotus pond, and recite this essence a hundred thousand times. At the end, one should make a moon out of moonstone or out of butter, place it among the petals of a lotus, and recite throughout the night. Then, at midnight or at sunrise, rays of light will appear, and once they have appeared they will enter the practitioner's mouth. Then, circling round and round like a wheel, they will come to rest all about him. Then he will succeed: he will be free of torment and will travel through the sky, and he will dwell for an eon in my celestial palace, abiding in the bliss of the gods. Glory too will be his, and whatever he desires, kingship and the like, will be granted."

Then all the planets, the Sun and the others, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, we have an essence called All Splendor and Strength. For the benefit and happiness of practitioners, and to give them long life and freedom from illness, we offer it here in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā siddhabedyodana svāhā

Blessed One, whatever practitioner recites this essence, no illness at all will arise in him. If he recites it constantly, he will become splendid. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should set up a lamp with five wicks on the sand of a riverbank and recite this essence a hundred thousand times. Then one should make a fire offering into the fire and recite until the sun sets. Then one of the suns will come. One should present him with the guest-water offering and the rest, and when he has accepted whatever he wishes, he will say, 'So be it,' and depart, and he will do whatever one desires. One may then go to any celestial palace whatever."

Then Varuṇa, king of the nāgas, together with many nāga kings, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, we too offer our own essence in this maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā apamapati biraja svāhā

Whoever engages in bodhisattva conduct through the gateway of secret mantra and recites this essence will become completely pure by nature. His understanding will become directly evident in all pure conduct. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should enter the great ocean until submerged up to the throat and recite this essence one million two hundred thousand times. Then, at the end, one should make a nāga out of the four kinds of precious substances, worship it with whatever one can afford, and recite until some nāga king or other is present. Then one should present the guest-water offering and say, 'Grant me whatever I wish.' From then on he will do whatever he is commanded. Entering that very place, one will dwell there for many years."

Then Viṣṇu, bowing with palms joined in the direction where the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was, said: "Blessed One, we too offer this our own essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā eka eka bara bara jvāla jvāla biṣṇabe svāhā

Blessed One, this is my essence. With it I repel all who are hostile. I destroy them. I put an end to all epidemics and all harms. Whoever remembers it constantly, his intelligence will not decline. If one wishes to accomplish this essence, one should go to a mountain peak and recite it five hundred thousand times. At the end, with earth from the top of an anthill mixed with the five products of the cow, one should make an image of Viṣṇu with four arms: in one left hand a discus, in one right hand a club, in the other left hand a conch, and the other right hand granting the supreme boon. One should make a maṇḍala with cow dung that has not fallen to the ground, worship with whatever one can afford, sit facing west, and recite until the image of Viṣṇu moves or the sound of a conch arises. Then one will succeed, and he will accomplish every purpose one has in mind."

Then Rudra, king of the hungry ghosts, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I have a supremely secret essence, and it is because I perform fierce deeds that all sentient beings call me Rudra, the Fierce One. Blessed One, with this essence I bring ruin upon the three worlds with all that moves and does not move. I bring them under my power. Blessed One, I offer this my essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hūṃ phaṭ

Blessed One, this essence of mine is a treasure for all. If anyone who engages in bodhisattva conduct through the gateway of secret mantra keeps it in mind, I will always follow after him. Together with all the hosts of hungry ghosts I will do as he commands. The three worlds too will come under his power. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should, in a charnel ground, smear the body with ashes, wear as an ornament a garland of human skull cups, hold a trident in the hand, and carry a khaṭvāṅga staff. One should wear a tiger-skin skirt, eat whatever alms food comes one's way, and recite this essence four hundred thousand times. Then, in a charnel ground where smoke billows, one should make a maṇḍala with cow dung that has not fallen to the ground, set down the trident and the khaṭvāṅga, make whatever offerings one can afford, burn human flesh as incense, and recite throughout the night. Then Rudra himself, surrounded by many hungry ghosts, will come in a terrifying form, dreadful to behold. One should not be afraid of him, and should present the guest-water offering. Then one will succeed. He will accomplish every purpose. He will follow after one and be eager to carry out one's commands."

Then Īśāna, lord of the bhūta spirits, bowed to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi and said: "Blessed One, I too offer my essence in this great maṇḍala.

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā ponnete datta bhūta adhipati svāhā

Blessed One, this essence of mine bestows every excellence. If any son or daughter of good family constantly keeps this essence in mind, I will always follow after that person. I will protect them. I will increase their glory. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should stay in a secluded garden, eating only in the evening, and recite this essence a hundred thousand times. At the end, one should make offerings with whatever one can afford, burn sandalwood incense, offer a vast torma, and offer eight butter lamps as well, and recite from sunset until midnight. Then one will succeed. All one's purposes will be accomplished. He will also bestow the elixir of rejuvenation."

Then the goddess Umā bowed to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi and said: "Blessed One, we too offer our own essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā umaye svāhā

Blessed One, this essence of mine brings about every good fortune. One should recite the mantra once over flowers, incense, eye salve, or bezoar, and then recite it. If one recites it constantly, one will become fortunate. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should accomplish it by applying the rite given for the essence of the Auspicious One in full, but doubled."

Then the great planet Kārttikeya, son of the Pleiades, said to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Blessed One, I too, with a mind full of joy, offer my own essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā he kumāra jvalita mūrti caru citra mabara svāhā

This essence of mine strikes terror into all. If anyone constantly keeps it in mind, no fear at all will arise for him. None of the grahas that seek an opening will find one. One should empower a peacock's tail feather with the mantra and use it to treat someone seized by a graha; if one also burns it as incense, he will recover. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should first honor eight young boys with great respect and give them a morning meal, giving them whatever food they crave. Then, in a shrine of the mātṛkās, holding a short spear in one's hand and in the guise of a youth, one should recite this essence a hundred thousand times. At the end, in that same maṇḍala of the mātṛkās, one should make offerings with whatever one can afford, make an image out of clay taken from both banks of a river, plant the short spear in its heart, and recite until a sound rings out from the earth saying, 'He is dead!' or 'His back is broken!' Then one will succeed. From then on he will do whatever one wishes."

Then the god White Cloud, together with all the cloud gods, bowed with palms joined toward where the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was, and said: "Blessed One, we too offer this our own essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā gulu gulu garja garja mahāsbudbhaba bara bindu tutmāla dar dar pura pura kasa kasa svāhā

This essence of ours benefits all beings. By means of it we cause the expanse of the sky to be covered with a vast, dense net of clouds and bring all crops to ripeness. We also satisfy this great earth with a great stream of rain. Blessed One, if any bodhisattva who engages in bodhisattva conduct through the gateway of secret mantra constantly keeps it in mind, he will have no fear of lacking water. No illness at all will arise in his body. All his wishes will be completely fulfilled. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should, filled with loving-kindness, recite for six months, from moonrise until moonrise. When the six months are complete, one should make offerings with whatever one can afford, take water from the four great oceans, and fill a vase whose base has not been blackened. One should worship it with perfume, flowers and incense, and recite until foam rises from the water and sprays upward and a rumbling sound or the sound of thunder rings out. Then some rain-cloud god or other will come and scatter the water into the sky. Then one will succeed, and he will accomplish every purpose as one wishes."

Then those called Cloud-Crushing Lightning, Lightning Splendor, Lightning Radiance, and Lightning Fine Garland bowed with palms joined toward where the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was, and said: "Blessed One, in order to pacify all harms, we too offer our own essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā dhāraya dhāraya sphura sarva mahāpritikara svāhā

Blessed One, if anyone keeps this essence in mind, we will protect him. We will shelter and conceal him. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should light lamps in the four directions, fumigate the four directions with incense, make offerings with whatever one can afford, place four vases in the four directions, each of the color proper to its direction, and recite throughout the night. Then, when lightning appears in the four directions, one should not be afraid, and if one presents the guest-water offering to all of them, one will succeed. They will lead one to their own abode and protect one. They will accomplish whatever one has in mind. They will grant whatever supreme thing one desires."

Then the great kings of mountains, Meru, king of mountains, and the others, bowed with palms joined toward where the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was, and said: "Blessed One, for the sake of the bodhisattvas who engage in bodhisattva conduct through the gateway of secret mantra, we too offer our own essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā cala cala mahīdhara ratnadhara svāhā

Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps this essence of ours in mind, when he goes into the wilderness we will protect, shelter and conceal him. We will also provide all the requisites for his comfort.

If one wishes to accomplish it, one should climb to a mountain peak and recite this essence a hundred thousand times. Then one should make offerings with whatever one can afford and perform a fire offering ten thousand times with oblations mixed with milk, curd, honey and butter. Then one of us mountain kings will come and say, 'Son, come here! Let us go to our abode. I will give you whatever you wish.' And the practitioner should say, 'Whenever I need you and call you to mind, come to me!' Then he will say, 'So be it! I will do everything. Do not beg from anyone else.'"

Then Yama, Kālarātri, Kāladaṇḍa, Kālapuruṣa, Kālakarṇī and Glory-Giver bowed to the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi and said: "Blessed One, we too offer each our own essence in this great maṇḍala:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vaibhāsatvāya svāhā

Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps this essence of ours in mind, I will not send my servants and retinue to kill him or to summon him. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should fast on the fourteenth or the eighth day of the waning fortnight, make from the flour of ground śāli rice an image of Yama, eight or six finger-widths high, together with his retinue, and place a buffalo and a club on either side. Then one should make offerings with whatever one can afford and recite throughout the night. At midnight or at sunrise, the buffalo will make a sound or the club will move. Then one should present the guest-water offering and the rest, and ask what one wishes. Then one will succeed. He will perform the actions one desires."

Then Kālarātri spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bikarāle rātrimāte svāhā

"Whoever constantly keeps it in mind, I will protect him. If one wishes to accomplish it, it is as before."

Kāladaṇḍa too spoke his own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā tamodabhavā kālapāśaga svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps it in mind, I will not go near him. I will also bring him benefit. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should accomplish it as before, and I will completely fulfill all his wishes."

Then Kālapuruṣa spoke his own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā uttamaburuṣa svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps this essence of mine in mind, Blessed One, I will not go near him, but will bring him benefit. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should accomplish it as before. There is this slight difference: one should set out two nooses made of hair. All one's wishes will be completely fulfilled."

Then Mṛtyu, Lord of Death, spoke his own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā apratihatakade karmmabaśa abukedebe barade svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps this essence of mine in mind, I will not go near him, except as his past karma decrees. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should accomplish it as before, and set out a bell."

Then the Hero-Maker spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā bākṛti mahātraitye bhagavati muti mukhi svāhā

"Whoever constantly keeps it in mind, I will not go near him, except for one who slanders the noble ones, rejects the true Dharma, or reviles the guru. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should accomplish it as before, and set out salted rice porridge. She will bestow every excellence."

Then Kālakarṇī spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kālacakraṇi svāhā

"Whoever constantly keeps it in mind, I will not go near him, except for one who has displeased his father and mother. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should accomplish it as before. In particular, one should set out a brazier holding a fire of chaff. Whoever does this, I will always follow after him."

Then all the mātṛkās bowed with palms joined toward where the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi was, and said: "Blessed One, we fierce mātṛkās too, in order to benefit the world, will dwell in every village and every region. We offer each our own essence in this great maṇḍala."

Then the consort of Brahmā spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā oṃ brahmapatini svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps this essence in mind, I will always follow after him. All the mātṛkās too will rejoice and always protect him. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should visualize the syllable oṃ at one's heart and recite a hundred thousand times. Then one should make a maṇḍala of the mātṛkās, worship with whatever one can afford, and count the mantra throughout the night. Holding a jewel and a round water pot in one's hands, one should recite until the sound of the syllable oṃ rings out. Then one will succeed, and she will perform whatever action one wishes. She will grant whatever one wishes."

Then the consort of Rudra spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā hrīḥ phaṭ svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever continually keeps this essence of mine in mind, I will always follow after him and do as he commands. I will grant whatever he has in mind. If one wishes to accomplish it, one should eat alms food, stay the night in a charnel ground, and recite two hundred thousand times at the right side of an image of the Great God (Śiva). At the end, one should worship with whatever one can afford, and perform a fire offering of forty thousand datukeraka flowers dipped in curd, honey and butter into the fire of the charnel ground. At midnight the sound of a bull or the sound of the syllable oṃ will ring out, and then all the mātṛkās will have been accomplished.

Then the consort of Viṣṇu spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā nārayani jagadathi hari bala badi jaye svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever constantly keeps this essence in mind, I will always follow after him in order to protect, conceal and shelter him. If one wishes to accomplish this essence, one should recite it fifty thousand times at a place where merit flows down. At the end, on the twelfth day of the last month of autumn, one should worship with whatever one can afford and perform a fire offering ten thousand times with mandārava flowers dipped in butter. Then one will succeed, and she will grant whatever one wishes. One will gain the pledge of all the mātṛkās."

Then the consort of Kumāra spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā kumāri mayūra citriketagadre śaktidhāriṇi bitci svāhā phu svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever keeps this essence in mind, I will follow after him and accomplish all his purposes. If one wishes to accomplish this essence, one should face south, hold a short spear in the left hand, and recite it four hundred thousand times. Then one should likewise worship with whatever one can afford and perform a fire offering throughout the night with grain sweetmeats each the size of a juniper berry. At midnight or in the morning, Kaumārī will come and stand before one, and one should present her with the guest-water offering. From then on one will have succeeded, and she will do whatever one wishes."

Then Cāmuṇḍī spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā rudhirabetāli ākarṣaṇi mahākṛtte cchinda cchinda bhinda bhinda camuṇḍi bitce svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever keeps this essence of mine in mind, I will always follow after him and accomplish every purpose he has in mind. If one wishes to accomplish this essence, one should wear blue clothing, hold a human skull in one's hand, eat meat as one's food for one month, and recite in a charnel ground or in a maṇḍala of the mātṛkās. At the end, in that same place, one should worship with whatever one can afford and perform a fire offering ten thousand times with oblations of human flesh dipped in white mustard oil. Then, uttering the sound 'ha ha,' licking her lips, and holding a human skull in her hand, she will come. One should present her with the guest-water offering in a human skull filled with blood. Then she will say, 'Excellent!' and, after circumambulating the practitioner, she will vanish. From then on she will always follow after one and do whatever one desires."

Then the consort of the Boar spoke her own essence:

namaḥ sarvatathāgatebhyaḥ sarvamukhebhyaḥ sarvathā vajragoṇinibīdavajrotirabīrabīralihūṃkari svāhā

"Blessed One, whoever keeps this essence in mind, I will always follow after him and accomplish all his purposes.
